1	---
2	component: Group
3	category: utility
4	tier: stateless
5	foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]

10	---
11	
12	# Group
13	
14	A general-purpose grouping component that propagates `disabled`, `invalid`, and `read_only` state to all descendant components via context. Renders as a `<div>` with `role="group"` (or `role="region"` / `role="presentation"`).
15	
16	Unlike `Fieldset`, which renders `<fieldset>`/`<legend>` and is form-specific, Group is a lightweight wrapper for any set of related controls (e.g., a group of buttons sharing a disabled state, or a NumberField's input + increment/decrement button cluster).
17	
18	**Ark UI equivalent:** — (no direct equivalent)
19	**React Aria equivalent:** Group
20	
21	## 1. API
22	
23	### 1.1 Props
24	
25	```rust
26	use ars_i18n::Direction;
27	
28	/// The ARIA role applied to the group container.
29	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
30	pub enum GroupRole {
31	    /// `role="group"` — a set of related UI elements not important enough
32	    /// to be included in a page summary or table of contents.
33	    #[default]
34	    Group,
35	    /// `role="region"` — a landmark region that is significant enough to be
36	    /// listed in a page summary. Requires an accessible name.
37	    Region,
38	    /// `role="presentation"` — removes the grouping semantics. Children are
39	    /// still grouped visually but not semantically.
40	    Presentation,
41	}
42	
43	/// Props for the `Group` component.
44	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]
45	pub struct Props {
46	    /// Component instance ID.
47	    pub id: String,
48	    /// Whether the group and all contained controls are disabled.
49	    /// Propagated to children via `GroupContext`.
50	    pub disabled: bool,
51	    /// Whether the group is in an invalid state.
52	    /// Propagated to children via `GroupContext`.
53	    pub invalid: bool,
54	    /// Whether the group is read-only.
55	    /// Propagated to children via `GroupContext`.
56	    pub read_only: bool,
57	    /// The ARIA role for the group container. Default: `Group`.
58	    pub role: GroupRole,
59	    /// Layout direction for RTL support.
60	    pub dir: Option<Direction>,
61	}
62	
63	impl Default for Props {
64	    fn default() -> Self {
65	        Self {
66	            id: String::new(),
67	            disabled: false,
68	            invalid: false,
69	            read_only: false,
70	            role: GroupRole::default(),
71	            dir: None,
72	        }
73	    }
74	}
75	```
76	
77	### 1.2 Connect / API
78	
79	```rust
80	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
81	#[scope = "group"]
82	pub enum Part {
83	    Root,
84	}
85	
86	/// Context propagated to descendant components.
87	/// Children read this to inherit disabled/invalid/read_only state.
88	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
89	pub struct GroupContext {
90	    /// Whether the group is disabled.
91	    pub disabled: bool,
92	    /// Whether the group is in an invalid state.
93	    pub invalid: bool,
94	    /// Whether the group is read-only.
95	    pub read_only: bool,
96	}
97	
98	/// The API for the `Group` component.
99	pub struct Api<'a> {
100	    props: &'a Props,
101	}
102	
103	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
104	    pub fn new(props: &'a Props) -> Self {
105	        Self { props }
106	    }
107	
108	    /// Returns the `GroupContext` to provide to descendants.
109	    pub fn group_context(&self) -> GroupContext {
110	        GroupContext {
111	            disabled: self.props.disabled,
112	            invalid: self.props.invalid,
113	            read_only: self.props.read_only,
114	        }
115	    }
116	
117	    /// Root element attributes.
118	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
119	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
120	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.props.id);
121	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
122	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
123	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
124	
125	        // Role
126	        match self.props.role {
127	            GroupRole::Group => attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "group"),
128	            GroupRole::Region => attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "region"),
129	            GroupRole::Presentation => attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "presentation"),
130	        }
131	
132	        // State attributes
133	        if self.props.disabled {
134	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), "true");
135	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true);
136	        }
137	        if self.props.invalid {
138	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid), "true");
139	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-invalid"), true);
140	        }
141	        if self.props.read_only {
142	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), "true");
143	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true);
144	        }
145	        if let Some(dir) = self.props.dir {
146	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Dir, dir.as_html_attr());
147	        }
148	
149	        attrs
150	    }
151	}
152	
153	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
154	    type Part = Part;
155	
156	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
157	        match part {
158	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
159	        }
160	    }
161	}
162	```
163	
164	## 2. Anatomy
165	
166	```text
167	Group
168	├── Root        <div>  data-ars-scope="group" data-ars-part="root" role="group"
169	└── {children}         Descendant components inherit state via GroupContext
170	```
171	
172	| Part | Element | Key Attributes                                                                            |
173	| ---- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
174	| Root | `<div>` | `data-ars-scope="group"`, `data-ars-part="root"`, `role`, `aria-disabled`, `aria-invalid` |
175	
176	## 3. Accessibility
177	
178	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
179	
180	| Attribute          | Element | Source            | Notes                                                        |
181	| ------------------ | ------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
182	| `role`             | Root    | `props.role`      | `"group"` (default), `"region"`, or `"presentation"`         |
183	| `aria-disabled`    | Root    | `props.disabled`  | `"true"` when the group is disabled                          |
184	| `aria-invalid`     | Root    | `props.invalid`   | `"true"` when the group is in an invalid state               |
185	| `aria-readonly`    | Root    | `props.read_only` | `"true"` when the group is read-only                         |
186	| `aria-label`       | Root    | Consumer-provided | Accessible name for the group (required for `role="region"`) |
187	| `aria-labelledby`  | Root    | Consumer-provided | Alternative accessible name via referenced element           |
188	| `aria-describedby` | Root    | Consumer-provided | Links to description or error message                        |
189	
190	- When `role` is `Region`, an accessible name (`aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`) is **required** per WAI-ARIA.
191	- When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`, `aria-invalid`) are omitted since the grouping has no semantic meaning.
192	- `aria-disabled` on a `role="group"` container does NOT natively propagate to children (unlike `<fieldset disabled>`). The adapter MUST use `GroupContext` to disable children programmatically.
193	
194	### 3.2 Context Propagation
195	
196	Adapters MUST provide `GroupContext` via the framework context system (`provide_context` in Leptos, `use_context_provider` in Dioxus). Child components that support disabling (Button, TextField, etc.) SHOULD read from `GroupContext` and merge with their own props:
197	
198	```rust,no_check
199	// Inside a child component's adapter:
200	let group_ctx = use_context::<GroupContext>();
201	let effective_disabled = props.disabled || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.disabled);
202	let effective_invalid = props.invalid || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.invalid);
203	let effective_read_only = props.read_only || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.read_only);
204	```
205	
206	A component's own props always take precedence — if a component explicitly sets `disabled=false`, it is NOT overridden by the group's `disabled=true`. The merge uses logical OR: disabled if either the component or the group is disabled.
207	
208	## 4. Internationalization
209	
210	- In RTL layouts, the `dir` prop is forwarded to the root element. Child components inherit direction from the DOM cascade.
211	- Group has no text content and requires no localization strings.
212	
213	## 5. Library Parity
214	
215	> Compared against: React Aria (`Group`).
216	
217	### 5.1 Props
218	
219	| Feature   | ars-ui            | React Aria   | Notes                                            |
220	| --------- | ----------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
221	| Disabled  | `disabled`        | `isDisabled` | Both libraries                                   |
222	| Invalid   | `invalid`         | `isInvalid`  | Both libraries                                   |
223	| Read-only | `read_only`       | `isReadOnly` | Both libraries                                   |
224	| Role      | `role: GroupRole` | `role`       | Both libraries; ars-ui uses enum, RA uses string |
225	| Dir       | `dir`             | --           | ars-ui addition                                  |
226	
227	**Gaps:** None.
228	
229	### 5.2 Anatomy
230	
231	| Part | ars-ui | React Aria | Notes                       |
232	| ---- | ------ | ---------- | --------------------------- |
233	| Root | `Root` | `Group`    | Both libraries; single-part |
234	
235	**Gaps:** None.
236	
237	### 5.3 Events
238	
239	| Callback     | ars-ui | React Aria                | Notes                      |
240	| ------------ | ------ | ------------------------- | -------------------------- |
241	| Hover events | --     | `onHoverStart/End/Change` | RA exposes hover callbacks |
242	
243	**Gaps:** None. Hover events for a grouping container are low-priority and not adopted.
244	
245	### 5.4 Summary
246	
247	- **Overall:** Full parity.
248	- **Divergences:** React Aria exposes hover callbacks on Group; ars-ui omits them since Group is a state-propagation wrapper, not an interactive element.
249	- **Recommended additions:** None.
250

Excellent. Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile my findings:

## EXPLORATION REPORT: Group Component Implementation

### 1. Stateless Utility Component Templates

I examined five similar stateless components. **Separator is the closest structural match:**

| Component | Lines | Structure | State Machine |
|-----------|-------|-----------|---------------|
| **Separator** | 446 | Props → Part(Root) → Api → ConnectApi | None (pure Api) |
| **Landmark** | 756 | Props → Part(Root) → Api + Messages → ConnectApi | None |
| **VisuallyHidden** | 404 | Props → Part(Root) → Api → ConnectApi | None |
| **FocusRing** | 604 | Props → Context(runtime) → Part(Root) → Api → ConnectApi | None |
| **Highlight** | ~200+ | Props (no id) → Part(Root) → Api (stateless chunks) | None |

**Separator** is your template: 446 lines, Props with id/HasId, single Part::Root with #[scope="separator"], builder chain, stateless Api with root_attrs(), ConnectApi impl, comprehensive test suite with snapshot tests. Group matches this pattern exactly except Group adds a Context struct for child propagation.

### 2. Utility Module Registration

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` (lines 1–65)

Pattern: Each utility is exposed as `pub mod {name}`. Group will be added as:
```rust
/// `Group` component (state-propagation wrapper for disabled/invalid/read_only).
pub mod group;
```

### 3. Part, ConnectApi, ComponentPart Usage Pattern

From **separator.rs** (lines 10–12, 62–69, 126–152, 154–162):

```rust
use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr};

#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "separator"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
}

pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
    // ... state-specific logic
    attrs
}

impl ConnectApi for Api {
    type Part = Part;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
        }
    }
}
```

### 4. AttrMap Methods

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-core/src/connect.rs` (lines 1–320+)

All required variants exist:
- **HtmlAttr::Id** (line 268)
- **HtmlAttr::Role** (line 303)
- **HtmlAttr::Dir** (line 255)
- **HtmlAttr::Data(&'static str)** (line 231) — for data-ars-* attributes
- **HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr)** (line 234)
  - **AriaAttr::Disabled** (line 25)
  - **AriaAttr::Invalid** (line 39)
  - **AriaAttr::ReadOnly** (line 72) ✓ exists
  - **AriaAttr::Label** (line 45)
  - **AriaAttr::LabelledBy** (line 48)

Methods: `attrs.set()` and `attrs.set_bool()` used throughout (e.g., focus_ring.rs line 217).

### 5. Context Propagation Patterns

Field has a Context struct defined in `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-components/src/utility/field/mod.rs` (lines 58–87):

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Context {
    pub required: bool,
    pub disabled: bool,
    pub readonly: bool,
    pub invalid: bool,
    pub validating: bool,
    pub dir: Option<Direction>,
    pub errors: Vec<Error>,
    pub has_description: bool,
    pub ids: ComponentIds,
}
```

GroupContext follows this pattern exactly: `#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]` with three propagated state fields. **No Messages needed** (unlike Landmark) since Group has no localization requirement.

### 6. Snapshot Tests

**Location:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/`

Naming pattern: `ars_components__utility__{component}__tests__{test_name}.snap`

Example Separator snapshots (from find output):
- `ars_components__utility__separator__tests__separator_root_horizontal.snap`
- `ars_components__utility__separator__tests__separator_root_decorative.snap`

Group snapshots will follow: `ars_components__utility__group__tests__group_root_{role_variant}.snap`

### 7. CRITICAL FINDING: Spec vs. Issue Contradiction

**Issue:** "Tests to add first" section mentions tests for `label` → `aria-label` and `label_id` → `aria-labelledby`.

**Spec at §3.1 (lines 186–188):** Shows aria-label and aria-labelledby in the accessibility table BUT marks them as **"Consumer-provided"** — meaning they come from the adapter, NOT from Group props.

**Group spec has NO label or label_id fields** in Props (lines 23–61). The spec explicitly states (lines 186–188):
```
| `aria-label`       | Root    | Consumer-provided | Accessible name for the group...
| `aria-labelledby`  | Root    | Consumer-provided | Alternative accessible name...
```

**The contradiction:** The issue task asks for tests on non-existent props. The spec says these attributes are "Consumer-provided" (set by the user spreading attributes, not by Group itself). This mismatch must be resolved before implementation. Either:
- Add `label` and `label_id` props to Group (contradicting the spec's "Consumer-provided" notation), OR
- Document in the issue that Group does NOT provide label/label_id API and adapters must handle labeling via prop spreading.
```

## Test Conventions Report: ars-ui Components

### 1. Snapshot Test Conventions

**Location:** In-file `#[cfg(test)]` blocks in component source files.
- **Example:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs` lines 164-445
- **Snapshot files:** Stored in `snapshots/` subdirectory alongside component module
  - `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/ars_components__utility__separator__tests__separator_root_horizontal.snap`
  - `/crates/ars-components/src/input/checkbox/snapshots/ars_components__input__checkbox__tests__checkbox_control_disabled_readonly_invalid.snap`

**Snapshot macro calls:**
```rust
use insta::assert_snapshot;

#[test]
fn separator_root_horizontal_snapshot() {
    assert_snapshot!(
        "separator_root_horizontal",
        snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs())
    );
}
```

**Helper function pattern** (shared across all components):
```rust
fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
    format!("{attrs:#?}")  // Debug format of AttrMap
}
```

**Naming convention:** `{component}_{part}_{state}_snapshot` (e.g., `separator_root_horizontal_snapshot`, `checkbox_control_disabled_readonly_invalid_snapshot`).

**No advanced insta features:** Plain `assert_snapshot!` with inline snapshot names—no `with_settings!` blocks, `sort_maps`, or custom paths observed.

**Snapshot format:** Plain Rust `Debug` output (YAML not used; only `insta = { version = "1", features = ["yaml"] }` in Cargo.toml but snapshots are `.snap` text files with `---` YAML metadata header).

---

### 2. Unit Tests for State-based Components

Separator (stateless utility) test coverage:

1. **Props validation**: default values, builder pattern, field overrides, Clone, PartialEq, HasId derive
2. **Api accessors**: id(), orientation(), decorative() return correct values
3. **ConnectApi dispatch**: part_attrs(Part::Root) matches inherent root_attrs()
4. **Semantic behavior**: role="separator" + aria-orientation for non-decorative; role="none" for decorative
5. **Orientation branching**: horizontal/vertical produce different attrs; decorative orientation is ignored
6. **Cross-branch assertions**: semantic ≠ decorative; horizontal ≠ vertical
7. **Snapshot tests**: one per output path (horizontal, vertical, decorative)

**For Group**, based on Landmark/Separator patterns, test:
- ✓ ConnectApi produces role="group" (default)
- ✓ role prop overrides to region/presentation via enum variants
- ✓ GroupContext propagates disabled, invalid, read_only (via Service state machine, not separator's stateless pattern)
- ✓ dir prop produces dir attribute
- ✓ Combined disabled+invalid+read_only state attrs (snapshot test per state combo)

---

### 3. Insta Dependency Setup

**Location:** `/crates/ars-components/Cargo.toml` lines 38-40:
```toml
[dev-dependencies]
insta    = { version = "1", features = ["yaml"] }
proptest = "1"
```

No root `Cargo.toml` override; inherited by workspace crates.

---

### 4. Snapshot Workflow

**Command:** `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject` expects:
- Snapshot files committed alongside source in `src/{category}/snapshots/*.snap`
- Naming pattern: `ars_components__{category}__{module}__tests__{test_name}.snap`
- `.snap` files contain YAML metadata header + Debug output
- Uncommitted snapshots are rejected by `--unreferenced=reject` flag

---

### 5. Doc Tests

**None found.** Separator and Landmark ship **unit tests only**, not doc tests. Doc comments use `///` but contain no code examples (only `[`type`]` cross-references).

---

### 6. Coverage Thresholds

**Location:** `/xtask/src/coverage.rs` lines 54–75:

```rust
CrateThreshold {
    package: "ars-components".into(),
    min_line: 90.0,
    min_branch: 90.0,
},
```

**ars-components global threshold:** 90% line coverage, 90% branch coverage. No per-module overrides; threshold is crate-wide.

**Current baseline (line 42):** 90.5 / 92.3 — already met; focus on maintaining branch coverage.

---

**Bottom line for Group:** Write `#[cfg(test)]` unit tests with 3–5 snapshot tests (one per major state combo) using `assert_snapshot!("group_{state}", snapshot_attrs(...))`, store `.snap` in `src/utility/snapshots/`, and aim for 90%+ coverage on ConnectApi and state handling.

1	//! `Separator` component machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! `Separator` is a stateless attribute mapper that renders a horizontal or
4	//! vertical dividing line. It has no state machine — the framework-agnostic
5	//! core consists solely of `Props`, `Part`, and `Api`, which produces the
6	//! appropriate ARIA role and orientation attributes for the root element.
7	//!
8	//! See `spec/components/utility/separator.md` for the authoritative contract.
9	
10	use alloc::string::String;
11	
12	use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr};
13	use ars_i18n::Orientation;
14	
15	/// Props for the `Separator` component.
16	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]
17	pub struct Props {
18	    /// Component instance ID.
19	    pub id: String,
20	
21	    /// The orientation of the separator. Defaults to `Orientation::Horizontal`
22	    /// via `Orientation`'s own `Default` impl.
23	    pub orientation: Orientation,
24	
25	    /// Whether the separator is purely decorative and hidden from the
26	    /// accessibility tree.
27	    pub decorative: bool,
28	}
29	
30	impl Props {
31	    /// Returns fresh [`Props`] with the documented defaults — equivalent
32	    /// to [`Default::default`], offered as the entry point for the
33	    /// builder chain.
34	    #[must_use]
35	    pub fn new() -> Self {
36	        Self::default()
37	    }
38	
39	    /// Sets the component instance ID.
40	    #[must_use]
41	    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
42	        self.id = id.into();
43	        self
44	    }
45	
46	    /// Sets the layout orientation of the separator.
47	    #[must_use]
48	    pub const fn orientation(mut self, value: Orientation) -> Self {
49	        self.orientation = value;
50	        self
51	    }
52	
53	    /// Sets whether the separator is purely decorative (hidden from the
54	    /// accessibility tree).
55	    #[must_use]
56	    pub const fn decorative(mut self, value: bool) -> Self {
57	        self.decorative = value;
58	        self
59	    }
60	}
61	
62	/// DOM parts of the `Separator` component.
63	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
64	#[scope = "separator"]
65	pub enum Part {
66	    /// The root element. See spec §2.1 for adapter element-type selection
67	    /// (`<hr>` for content separators, `<div>` for menu/toolbar/listbox).
68	    Root,
69	}
70	
71	/// The API for the `Separator` component.
72	///
73	/// Constructed via [`Api::new`] from [`Props`] and queried via
74	/// [`Api::root_attrs`] or the [`ConnectApi`] dispatch.
75	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
76	pub struct Api {
77	    props: Props,
78	}
79	
80	impl Api {
81	    /// Creates a new `Api` instance from the given props.
82	    #[must_use]
83	    pub const fn new(props: Props) -> Self {
84	        Self { props }
85	    }
86	
87	    /// Returns a reference to the underlying [`Props`].
88	    ///
89	    /// Adapters typically read individual fields through the dedicated
90	    /// accessors (`id`, `orientation`, `decorative`); this method is the
91	    /// escape hatch for when the full struct is needed (e.g., to clone it
92	    /// into a fresh [`Api`] for a re-render).
93	    #[must_use]
94	    pub const fn props(&self) -> &Props {
95	        &self.props
96	    }
97	
98	    /// Returns the component's instance ID.
99	    #[must_use]
100	    pub fn id(&self) -> &str {
101	        &self.props.id
102	    }
103	
104	    /// Returns the layout orientation of the separator.
105	    #[must_use]
106	    pub const fn orientation(&self) -> Orientation {
107	        self.props.orientation
108	    }
109	
110	    /// Returns whether the separator is purely decorative.
111	    #[must_use]
112	    pub const fn decorative(&self) -> bool {
113	        self.props.decorative
114	    }
115	
116	    /// Returns the attributes for the root element.
117	    ///
118	    /// Semantic separators get `role="separator"` plus `aria-orientation`
119	    /// matching the layout axis, and `data-ars-orientation` for styling.
120	    /// Decorative separators get `role="none"` (the modern WAI-ARIA 1.2
121	    /// preferred form, synonymous with `role="presentation"`); they omit
122	    /// `aria-orientation` and the `data-ars-orientation` styling hook,
123	    /// because a decorative separator is invisible to assistive technology
124	    /// and component-managed orientation styling is not applied.
125	    #[must_use]
126	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
127	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
128	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
129	
130	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
131	
132	        if self.props.decorative {
133	            // Decorative separators are removed from the accessibility tree
134	            // via `role="none"`. No `aria-hidden` (redundant for an element
135	            // with no children) and no `data-ars-orientation` (decorative
136	            // separators do not participate in component orientation styling).
137	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "none");
138	        } else {
139	            let orientation_str = match self.props.orientation {
140	                Orientation::Horizontal => "horizontal",
141	                Orientation::Vertical => "vertical",
142	            };
143	
144	            attrs
145	                .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation"), orientation_str)
146	                .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "separator")
147	                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Orientation), orientation_str);
148	        }
149	
150	        attrs
151	    }
152	}
153	
154	impl ConnectApi for Api {
155	    type Part = Part;
156	
157	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
158	        match part {
159	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
160	        }
161	    }
162	}
163

1	//! Field component state machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! This module implements the framework-agnostic `Field` machine defined in
4	//! `spec/foundation/07-forms.md` §13. The machine is intentionally stateless
5	//! at the state-enum level and instead tracks required, validation, and ARIA
6	//! wiring details in its context.
7	
8	use alloc::{string::String, vec::Vec};
9	use core::fmt::{self, Debug};
10	
11	use ars_core::{
12	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentIds, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Direction, Env, HtmlAttr,
13	    TransitionPlan,
14	};
15	use ars_forms::validation::Error;
16	
17	/// Single state for the field component machine.
18	///
19	/// `Field` is effectively stateless. All meaningful changes are stored in
20	/// [`Context`] and applied through context-only transitions.
21	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
22	pub enum State {
23	    /// The field is mounted and ready to expose its current context.
24	    Idle,
25	}
26	
27	/// Events that update field component context.
28	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
29	pub enum Event {
30	    /// Replaces the current validation errors.
31	    SetErrors(Vec<Error>),
32	
33	    /// Clears all current validation errors.
34	    ClearErrors,
35	
36	    /// Tracks whether a description part is rendered.
37	    SetHasDescription(bool),
38	
39	    /// Synchronizes the disabled state from props.
40	    SetDisabled(bool),
41	
42	    /// Synchronizes the invalid state from props.
43	    SetInvalid(bool),
44	
45	    /// Synchronizes the readonly state from props.
46	    SetReadonly(bool),
47	
48	    /// Synchronizes the required state from props.
49	    SetRequired(bool),
50	
51	    /// Synchronizes the layout direction from props.
52	    SetDir(Option<Direction>),
53	
54	    /// Tracks whether async validation is currently running.
55	    SetValidating(bool),
56	}
57	
58	/// Mutable machine context for the field component.
59	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
60	pub struct Context {
61	    /// Whether the field is required.
62	    pub required: bool,
63	
64	    /// Whether the field is disabled.
65	    pub disabled: bool,
66	
67	    /// Whether the field is read-only.
68	    pub readonly: bool,
69	
70	    /// Whether the field is currently invalid.
71	    pub invalid: bool,
72	
73	    /// Whether an async validator is currently running.
74	    pub validating: bool,
75	
76	    /// The configured text direction for RTL-aware rendering.
77	    pub dir: Option<Direction>,
78	
79	    /// Field-level validation errors.
80	    pub errors: Vec<Error>,
81	
82	    /// Whether a description part is rendered and should be referenced by ARIA.
83	    pub has_description: bool,
84	
85	    /// Stable IDs derived from the adapter-provided base ID.
86	    pub ids: ComponentIds,
87	}
88	
89	/// Immutable configuration for a field machine instance.
90	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
91	pub struct Props {
92	    /// Adapter-provided base ID for the field root.
93	    ///
94	    /// This ID is immutable for the lifetime of a machine instance because
95	    /// [`Context::ids`] caches the derived part IDs during initialization.
96	    pub id: String,
97	
98	    /// Whether the field is required.
99	    pub required: bool,
100	
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/plans/implement-ta[REDACTED_SK].md",
  "content": "# Plan: Implement task #205 — Group agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #205 (`task: Implement Group agnostic core`) is a **1-point** task in the\n`ars-ui implementation roadmap` project. It delivers the framework-agnostic core of the\n`Group` utility component: a stateless attribute mapper that renders a semantic grouping\ncontainer and exposes a `GroupContext` struct that adapters can publish to descendants for\n`disabled`/`invalid`/`read_only` propagation.\n\nAuthoritative spec: `spec/components/utility/group.md` (250 lines, fully specified —\n`tier: stateless`, `foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]`, no shared deps).\n\nStatus: `Todo` on the project board, no dependencies, no blockers.\n\n## Spec vs. issue discrepancy (RESOLVED — spec wins per CLAUDE.md \"spec is the contract\")\n\nThe issue's \"Tests to add first\" lists two tests that contradict the spec:\n\n- ❌ `` `label` prop produces `aria-label` ``\n- ❌ `` `label_id` prop produces `aria-labelledby` ``\n\nThe spec's `Props` struct (§1.1) has exactly six fields — `id`, `disabled`, `invalid`,\n`read_only`, `role`, `dir` — and **no** `label`/`label_id`. Spec §3.1 explicitly marks\n`aria-label` and `aria-labelledby` as \"Consumer-provided\", meaning adapter users supply them\nvia prop spreading, not via Group props. Inventing `label`/`label_id` props would silently\ndiverge from the React Aria parity table (§5.1) which already shows full parity without them.\n\n**Decision:** Implement per spec, drop the two contradicting test bullets, replace with\nspec-aligned tests below.\n\n## Internal spec inconsistency (resolved during implementation)\n\nSpec §3.1 prose: \"When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`,\n`aria-invalid`) are omitted since the grouping has no semantic meaning.\"\n\nSpec §1.2 code example (lines 132–147): unconditionally emits state attributes regardless of\n`role`. The code example matches what `Separator` does for its `decorative` analog.\n\n**Decision:** Implement the **code example** (always emit when prop is true) — that is the\ncanonical spec contract per CLAUDE.md \"match the spec code exactly\". The post-implementation\naudit will surface a parallel spec-text fix to update §3.1 prose to match the code, landing\nin the same PR.\n\n## Files\n\n### Create\n\n- **`crates/ars-components/src/utility/group.rs`** — entire component module (Props, GroupRole,\n  GroupContext, Part, Api, ConnectApi impl, and `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`). Template:\n  `crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs` (446 lines, identical structure minus the\n  Context struct). No state machine, no Service, no Messages.\n\n### Modify\n\n- **`crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs`** — add one `pub mod group;` declaration\n  with a doc comment, alphabetically positioned (between `form_submit` and `heading`).\n\n### Snapshots (created by `cargo insta accept` after first test run)\n\n- `crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/ars_components__utility__group__tests__*.snap`\n  (committed as part of this PR; PR gets the `snapshot-reviewed` label per CLAUDE.md).\n\n## Implementation outline\n\nMatch `spec/components/utility/group.md` §1.1 and §1.2 line-for-line. Specifically:\n\n1. **`GroupRole`** — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]` enum with three\n   variants (`Group` default, `Region`, `Presentation`). Use `#[default]` on `Group`.\n\n2. **`Props`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]`. The spec writes a manual\n   `impl Default` that produces identical output to derive; use derive for DRY (matches\n   `Separator`'s convention). Builder chain matching separator's pattern: `new()`, `id()`,\n   `disabled()`, `invalid()`, `read_only()`, `role()`, `dir()`. Setters that don't take\n   `String` are `const fn`.\n\n3. **`GroupContext`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]` plain struct. Three `pub bool`\n   fields exactly as spec §1.2. Named `GroupContext` (not `Context`) per foundation 08 §8.4\n   (\"Secondary contexts or helpers use descriptive non-prefixed names — `GroupContext`,\n   `QueueContext`, ...\"), so consumers write `group::GroupContext`.\n\n4. **`Part`** — `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"group\"] pub enum Part { Root }`.\n\n5. **`Api`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug)] pub struct Api { props: Props }`. Methods:\n   `new(Props) -> Self` (`const`), `props() -> &Props`, `id() -> &str`,\n   `group_context() -> GroupContext`, `root_attrs() -> AttrMap`. `root_attrs` follows the\n   spec code: set Id, scope/part data attrs, then a `match` on role emitting\n   `role=\"group\"|\"region\"|\"presentation\"`, then three independent `if` blocks emitting\n   `aria-disabled` + `data-ars-disabled`, `aria-invalid` + `data-ars-invalid`,\n   `aria-readonly` + `data-ars-readonly`, then optional `dir`.\n\n6. **`ConnectApi for Api`** — single-arm `match part { Part::Root => self.root_attrs() }`.\n\n## Tests (TDD — write before implementation per workspace convention)\n\nDrop the issue's two label-related test bullets. The replacement coverage that fully\nexercises the spec contract:\n\n**Props / Api / Debug smoke**\n\n- `props_default_is_zero_state` — id=\"\", disabled/invalid/read_only=false, role=Group, dir=None.\n- `props_builder_round_trips` — chained setters set exactly their own fields; `Props::new()\n   == Props::default()`.\n- `props_has_id_derive_round_trips` — `with_id` / `set_id` / `HasId::id` round-trip.\n- `props_clone_and_partial_eq_round_trip`.\n- `props_and_api_debug_impl_is_non_empty`.\n- `api_id_returns_empty_str_for_default_props`.\n- `api_exposes_props_fields`.\n\n**Role mapping** (covers issue bullet 1 & 2)\n\n- `root_default_role_is_group` — `Api::new(Props::default()).root_attrs()` →\n  `role=\"group\"`.\n- `root_role_region_overrides` — `role(GroupRole::Region)` → `role=\"region\"`.\n- `root_role_presentation_overrides` — `role(GroupRole::Presentation)` → `role=\"presentation\"`.\n- `role_branches_produce_different_role_attrs` — cross-branch inequality\n  (defensive regression).\n\n**State propagation via `group_context()`** (covers issue bullet 3)\n\n- `group_context_propagates_disabled` — `Props { disabled: true, ..default }` →\n  `Api::group_context() == GroupContext { disabled: true, invalid: false, read_only: false }`.\n- `group_context_propagates_invalid` — analogous.\n- `group_context_propagates_read_only` — analogous.\n- `group_context_default_is_all_false`.\n\n**Root state attributes**\n\n- `root_disabled_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-disabled=\"true\"` + `data-ars-disabled` set.\n- `root_invalid_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-invalid=\"true\"` + `data-ars-invalid` set.\n- `root_read_only_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-readonly=\"true\"` + `data-ars-readonly` set.\n- `root_state_attrs_omitted_when_false` — none of the three are present in the default-props\n  AttrMap.\n- `root_dir_emits_dir_attr` — `dir(Some(Direction::Rtl))` → `dir=\"rtl\"`; default → no `dir`.\n- `root_id_is_set_to_props_id` — even when empty, follows spec §1.2 code which sets\n  unconditionally.\n\n**ConnectApi dispatch**\n\n- `part_attrs_dispatches_root` — sanity check (scope/part data attrs match).\n- `part_attrs_root_equals_root_attrs` — loop across role × disabled × invalid × read_only ×\n  dir combinations and assert `api.part_attrs(Part::Root) == api.root_attrs()`.\n\n**Snapshots** (one per output-affecting branch group, named per the\n`snapshot_attrs(&attr_map)` convention from separator.rs)\n\n- `group_root_default` (role=Group, no state)\n- `group_root_region` (role=Region, no state)\n- `group_root_presentation` (role=Presentation, no state)\n- `group_root_all_states` (role=Group, disabled+invalid+read_only=true)\n- `group_root_dir_rtl` (role=Group, dir=Rtl)\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `cargo test -p ars-components --lib utility::group` — all named tests pass.\n2. `cargo insta accept` (after first run) — review every `.snap` for spec accuracy, then\n   commit them; PR gets the `snapshot-reviewed` label.\n3. `cargo clippy -p ars-components --lib -- -D warnings` — zero warnings, in particular zero\n   `missing_docs` failures (all public items documented).\n4. `cargo doc -p ars-components --no-deps` — no doc warnings.\n5. `cargo llvm-cov test -p ars-components --text -- utility::group` — line and branch coverage\n   of new code at or above the 90/90 crate threshold; uncovered branches are a hard fail.\n6. **Run `post-implementation-audit` skill** (mandatory per CLAUDE.md §7) — three sequential\n   audits: (a) spec/impl drift incl. the §3.1 prose fix above, (b) iterative \"anything else\n   missing?\" rounds (≥2), (c) test-coverage audit across unit + snapshot + doctest dimensions.\n   Every finding lands in this PR.\n7. `cargo xci` — full pre-PR gate; must pass before opening the PR.\n8. Present diff to user → after approval, commit, push, open PR with `Closes #205` in the\n   body, attach `snapshot-reviewed` label.\n\n## Out of scope (deliberately deferred to follow-up issues)\n\n- Leptos adapter (`ars-leptos` Group component) — separate task per the project board.\n- Dioxus adapter (`ars-dioxus` Group component) — separate task per the project board.\n- Wiring `GroupContext` consumption into existing child components (Button, TextField,\n  etc.) — separate cross-component tasks once the adapter components exist.\n- Property tests under `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/` — Group has no\n  state transitions, so proptest's signal is low; unit + snapshot tests are the right\n  coverage tier for a stateless attribute mapper.\n\n## Critical files to reference during implementation\n\n| Purpose | Path |\n| --- | --- |\n| Authoritative contract | `spec/components/utility/group.md` |\n| Structural template | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs` |\n| Context-struct pattern (more complex example) | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/field/mod.rs` §lines 58–87 |\n| Module registration | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` |\n| Snapshot location convention | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/` |\n| Snapshot naming rule | `ars_components__utility__group__tests__{name}.snap` |\n| `Direction::as_html_attr` | `crates/ars-i18n/src/lib.rs:127` |\n| AttrMap / HtmlAttr / AriaAttr | `crates/ars-core/src/connect.rs` |\n| Foundation 08 §8.4 (naming) | `spec/foundation/08-adapter-leptos.md:2127` |\n| Coverage thresholds | `xtask/src/coverage.rs` (ars-components: 90/90) |\n"
}

  "new_string": "## Internal spec inconsistency — resolved on accessibility merits\n\nSpec §3.1 prose said: \"When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`,\n`aria-invalid`) are omitted since the grouping has no semantic meaning.\"\n\nSpec §1.2 code example: unconditionally emits state attributes regardless of `role`.\n\n**Best-outcome decision: always emit state attributes when the prop is true, regardless of\nrole.** Reasoning:\n\n1. **WAI-ARIA 1.2 §5.4** explicitly states `aria-*` global states and properties ARE still\n   exposed on elements with `role=\"presentation\"` — the role only suppresses the *implicit*\n   role mapping, not state attributes. The original §3.1 prose was based on a misreading.\n2. **React Aria parity** (§5.1 documents full parity): React Aria emits state attributes\n   unconditionally on its `Group`. Diverging here would silently break parity that the spec\n   commits to.\n3. **`GroupContext` is the load-bearing propagation mechanism**, not root ARIA. The root\n   `aria-disabled`/`aria-invalid`/`aria-readonly` are decorative complements that help AT\n   when the disabled state visually applies to the whole group; gating them on `role`\n   creates asymmetric behavior between the propagation path (always works) and the AT\n   announcement (sometimes works).\n4. **`data-ars-*` styling hooks must always emit** regardless of role so CSS targeting stays\n   consistent. Splitting aria-* vs data-* on role-gated logic introduces a footgun.\n5. **Simpler mental model**: `disabled=true ⇒ aria-disabled on root`, end of story. Fewer\n   branches, fewer edge cases, lower test surface.\n\n**Implementation consequence:** Match the §1.2 code example. **Spec consequence:** Replace\nthe §3.1 line \"When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`,\n`aria-invalid`) are omitted...\" with a brief note clarifying that state attributes are\nalways emitted (and citing the React Aria parity / WAI-ARIA §5.4 justification). Lands in\nthe same PR per CLAUDE.md \"no spec drift\" rule."
}

PLEASE IMPLEMENT THIS PLAN:
# Plan: Implement task #205 — Group agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #205 (`task: Implement Group agnostic core`) is a **1-point** task in the
`ars-ui implementation roadmap` project. It delivers the framework-agnostic core of the
`Group` utility component: a stateless attribute mapper that renders a semantic grouping
container and exposes a `GroupContext` struct that adapters can publish to descendants for
`disabled`/`invalid`/`read_only` propagation.

Authoritative spec: `spec/components/utility/group.md` (250 lines, fully specified —
`tier: stateless`, `foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]`, no shared deps).

Status: `Todo` on the project board, no dependencies, no blockers.

## Spec vs. issue discrepancy (RESOLVED — spec wins per CLAUDE.md "spec is the contract")

The issue's "Tests to add first" lists two tests that contradict the spec:

- ❌ `` `label` prop produces `aria-label` ``
- ❌ `` `label_id` prop produces `aria-labelledby` ``

The spec's `Props` struct (§1.1) has exactly six fields — `id`, `disabled`, `invalid`,
`read_only`, `role`, `dir` — and **no** `label`/`label_id`. Spec §3.1 explicitly marks
`aria-label` and `aria-labelledby` as "Consumer-provided", meaning adapter users supply them
via prop spreading, not via Group props. Inventing `label`/`label_id` props would silently
diverge from the React Aria parity table (§5.1) which already shows full parity without them.

**Decision:** Implement per spec, drop the two contradicting test bullets, replace with
spec-aligned tests below.

## Internal spec inconsistency — resolved on accessibility merits

Spec §3.1 prose said: "When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`,
`aria-invalid`) are omitted since the grouping has no semantic meaning."

Spec §1.2 code example: unconditionally emits state attributes regardless of `role`.

**Best-outcome decision: always emit state attributes when the prop is true, regardless of
role.** Reasoning:

1. **WAI-ARIA 1.2 §5.4** explicitly states `aria-*` global states and properties ARE still
   exposed on elements with `role="presentation"` — the role only suppresses the *implicit*
   role mapping, not state attributes. The original §3.1 prose was based on a misreading.
2. **React Aria parity** (§5.1 documents full parity): React Aria emits state attributes
   unconditionally on its `Group`. Diverging here would silently break parity that the spec
   commits to.
3. **`GroupContext` is the load-bearing propagation mechanism**, not root ARIA. The root
   `aria-disabled`/`aria-invalid`/`aria-readonly` are decorative complements that help AT
   when the disabled state visually applies to the whole group; gating them on `role`
   creates asymmetric behavior between the propagation path (always works) and the AT
   announcement (sometimes works).
4. **`data-ars-*` styling hooks must always emit** regardless of role so CSS targeting stays
   consistent. Splitting aria-* vs data-* on role-gated logic introduces a footgun.
5. **Simpler mental model**: `disabled=true ⇒ aria-disabled on root`, end of story. Fewer
   branches, fewer edge cases, lower test surface.

**Implementation consequence:** Match the §1.2 code example. **Spec consequence:** Replace
the §3.1 line "When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`,
`aria-invalid`) are omitted..." with a brief note clarifying that state attributes are
always emitted (and citing the React Aria parity / WAI-ARIA §5.4 justification). Lands in
the same PR per CLAUDE.md "no spec drift" rule.

## Files

### Create

- **`crates/ars-components/src/utility/group.rs`** — entire component module (Props, GroupRole,
  GroupContext, Part, Api, ConnectApi impl, and `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`). Template:
  `crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs` (446 lines, identical structure minus the
  Context struct). No state machine, no Service, no Messages.

### Modify

- **`crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs`** — add one `pub mod group;` declaration
  with a doc comment, alphabetically positioned (between `form_submit` and `heading`).
- **`spec/components/utility/group.md` §3.1** — replace the "When `role` is `Presentation`,
  state attributes are omitted..." line with a one-line note that state attributes are always
  emitted, citing WAI-ARIA 1.2 §5.4 and the React Aria parity commitment in §5.1. Keeps the
  spec internally consistent with the implementation per CLAUDE.md "Spec/code mismatch
  resolution".

### Snapshots (created by `cargo insta accept` after first test run)

- `crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/ars_components__utility__group__tests__*.snap`
  (committed as part of this PR; PR gets the `snapshot-reviewed` label per CLAUDE.md).

## Implementation outline

Match `spec/components/utility/group.md` §1.1 and §1.2 line-for-line. Specifically:

1. **`GroupRole`** — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]` enum with three
   variants (`Group` default, `Region`, `Presentation`). Use `#[default]` on `Group`.

2. **`Props`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]`. The spec writes a manual
   `impl Default` that produces identical output to derive; use derive for DRY (matches
   `Separator`'s convention). Builder chain matching separator's pattern: `new()`, `id()`,
   `disabled()`, `invalid()`, `read_only()`, `role()`, `dir()`. Setters that don't take
   `String` are `const fn`.

3. **`GroupContext`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]` plain struct. Three `pub bool`
   fields exactly as spec §1.2. Named `GroupContext` (not `Context`) per foundation 08 §8.4
   ("Secondary contexts or helpers use descriptive non-prefixed names — `GroupContext`,
   `QueueContext`, ..."), so consumers write `group::GroupContext`.

4. **`Part`** — `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "group"] pub enum Part { Root }`.

5. **`Api`** — `#[derive(Clone, Debug)] pub struct Api { props: Props }`. Methods:
   `new(Props) -> Self` (`const`), `props() -> &Props`, `id() -> &str`,
   `group_context() -> GroupContext`, `root_attrs() -> AttrMap`. `root_attrs` follows the
   spec code: set Id, scope/part data attrs, then a `match` on role emitting
   `role="group"|"region"|"presentation"`, then three independent `if` blocks emitting
   `aria-disabled` + `data-ars-disabled`, `aria-invalid` + `data-ars-invalid`,
   `aria-readonly` + `data-ars-readonly`, then optional `dir`.

6. **`ConnectApi for Api`** — single-arm `match part { Part::Root => self.root_attrs() }`.

## Tests (TDD — write before implementation per workspace convention)

Drop the issue's two label-related test bullets. The replacement coverage that fully
exercises the spec contract:

**Props / Api / Debug smoke**

- `props_default_is_zero_state` — id="", disabled/invalid/read_only=false, role=Group, dir=None.
- `props_builder_round_trips` — chained setters set exactly their own fields; `Props::new()
   == Props::default()`.
- `props_has_id_derive_round_trips` — `with_id` / `set_id` / `HasId::id` round-trip.
- `props_clone_and_partial_eq_round_trip`.
- `props_and_api_debug_impl_is_non_empty`.
- `api_id_returns_empty_str_for_default_props`.
- `api_exposes_props_fields`.

**Role mapping** (covers issue bullet 1 & 2)

- `root_default_role_is_group` — `Api::new(Props::default()).root_attrs()` →
  `role="group"`.
- `root_role_region_overrides` — `role(GroupRole::Region)` → `role="region"`.
- `root_role_presentation_overrides` — `role(GroupRole::Presentation)` → `role="presentation"`.
- `role_branches_produce_different_role_attrs` — cross-branch inequality
  (defensive regression).

**State propagation via `group_context()`** (covers issue bullet 3)

- `group_context_propagates_disabled` — `Props { disabled: true, ..default }` →
  `Api::group_context() == GroupContext { disabled: true, invalid: false, read_only: false }`.
- `group_context_propagates_invalid` — analogous.
- `group_context_propagates_read_only` — analogous.
- `group_context_default_is_all_false`.

**Root state attributes**

- `root_disabled_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-disabled="true"` + `data-ars-disabled` set.
- `root_invalid_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-invalid="true"` + `data-ars-invalid` set.
- `root_read_only_emits_aria_and_data_attrs` — `aria-readonly="true"` + `data-ars-readonly` set.
- `root_state_attrs_omitted_when_false` — none of the three are present in the default-props
  AttrMap.
- `root_dir_emits_dir_attr` — `dir(Some(Direction::Rtl))` → `dir="rtl"`; default → no `dir`.
- `root_id_is_set_to_props_id` — even when empty, follows spec §1.2 code which sets
  unconditionally.

**ConnectApi dispatch**

- `part_attrs_dispatches_root` — sanity check (scope/part data attrs match).
- `part_attrs_root_equals_root_attrs` — loop across role × disabled × invalid × read_only ×
  dir combinations and assert `api.part_attrs(Part::Root) == api.root_attrs()`.

**Snapshots** (one per output-affecting branch group, named per the
`snapshot_attrs(&attr_map)` convention from separator.rs)

- `group_root_default` (role=Group, no state)
- `group_root_region` (role=Region, no state)
- `group_root_presentation` (role=Presentation, no state)
- `group_root_all_states` (role=Group, disabled+invalid+read_only=true)
- `group_root_dir_rtl` (role=Group, dir=Rtl)

## Verification

1. `cargo test -p ars-components --lib utility::group` — all named tests pass.
2. `cargo insta accept` (after first run) — review every `.snap` for spec accuracy, then
   commit them; PR gets the `snapshot-reviewed` label.
3. `cargo clippy -p ars-components --lib -- -D warnings` — zero warnings, in particular zero
   `missing_docs` failures (all public items documented).
4. `cargo doc -p ars-components --no-deps` — no doc warnings.
5. `cargo llvm-cov test -p ars-components --text -- utility::group` — line and branch coverage
   of new code at or above the 90/90 crate threshold; uncovered branches are a hard fail.
6. **Run `post-implementation-audit` skill** (mandatory per CLAUDE.md §7) — three sequential
   audits: (a) spec/impl drift incl. the §3.1 prose fix above, (b) iterative "anything else
   missing?" rounds (≥2), (c) test-coverage audit across unit + snapshot + doctest dimensions.
   Every finding lands in this PR.
7. `cargo xci` — full pre-PR gate; must pass before opening the PR.
8. Present diff to user → after approval, commit, push, open PR with `Closes #205` in the
   body, attach `snapshot-reviewed` label.

## Out of scope (deliberately deferred to follow-up issues)

- Leptos adapter (`ars-leptos` Group component) — separate task per the project board.
- Dioxus adapter (`ars-dioxus` Group component) — separate task per the project board.
- Wiring `GroupContext` consumption into existing child components (Button, TextField,
  etc.) — separate cross-component tasks once the adapter components exist.
- Property tests under `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/` — Group has no
  state transitions, so proptest's signal is low; unit + snapshot tests are the right
  coverage tier for a stateless attribute mapper.

id: toolu_01EhtwiXEro19ws7du3arJmv
```
180	| Attribute          | Element | Source            | Notes                                                        |
181	| ------------------ | ------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
182	| `role`             | Root    | `props.role`      | `"group"` (default), `"region"`, or `"presentation"`         |
183	| `aria-disabled`    | Root    | `props.disabled`  | `"true"` when the group is disabled                          |
184	| `aria-invalid`     | Root    | `props.invalid`   | `"true"` when the group is in an invalid state               |
185	| `aria-readonly`    | Root    | `props.read_only` | `"true"` when the group is read-only                         |
186	| `aria-label`       | Root    | Consumer-provided | Accessible name for the group (required for `role="region"`) |
187	| `aria-labelledby`  | Root    | Consumer-provided | Alternative accessible name via referenced element           |
188	| `aria-describedby` | Root    | Consumer-provided | Links to description or error message                        |
189	
190	- When `role` is `Region`, an accessible name (`aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`) is **required** per WAI-ARIA.
191	- When `role` is `Presentation`, state attributes (`aria-disabled`, `aria-invalid`) are omitted since the grouping has no semantic meaning.
192	- `aria-disabled` on a `role="group"` container does NOT natively propagate to children (unlike `<fieldset disabled>`). The adapter MUST use `GroupContext` to disable children programmatically.
193	
194	### 3.2 Context Propagation
195	
196	Adapters MUST provide `GroupContext` via the framework context system (`provide_context` in Leptos, `use_context_provider` in Dioxus). Child components that support disabling (Button, TextField, etc.) SHOULD read from `GroupContext` and merge with their own props:
197	
198	```rust,no_check
199	// Inside a child component's adapter:
200	let group_ctx = use_context::<GroupContext>();
201	let effective_disabled = props.disabled || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.disabled);
202	let effective_invalid = props.invalid || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.invalid);
203	let effective_read_only = props.read_only || group_ctx.map_or(false, |g| g.read_only);
204	```
```

The adapter MUST use `GroupContext` to disable children programmatically.",
  "new_string": "- When `role` is `Region`, an accessible name (`aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`) is **required** per WAI-ARIA.\n- State attributes (`aria-disabled`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-readonly`) are emitted on the root whenever the corresponding prop is `true`, **regardless of `role`**. WAI-ARIA 1.2 §5.4 explicitly preserves global states and properties on elements with `role=\"presentation\"`; suppressing them there would only mask the visual state from assistive technology without changing the semantics. React Aria (parity reference, §5.1) also emits state attributes unconditionally.\n- `aria-disabled` on a `role=\"group\"` container does NOT natively propagate to children (unlike `<fieldset disabled>`). The adapter MUST use `GroupContext` to disable children programmatically."
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-e16f9343/crates/ars-components/src/utility/group.rs",
  "content": "//! `Group` component connect API.\n//!\n//! `Group` is a stateless semantic grouping wrapper. The framework-agnostic\n//! core consists of [`Props`], the [`GroupRole`] role selector, the [`Part`]\n//! taxonomy, [`Api`], and a [`GroupContext`] struct that adapters publish so\n//! descendant components can inherit `disabled`, `invalid`, and `read_only`\n//! state without re-implementing the propagation themselves.\n//!\n//! Unlike [`Fieldset`](super::fieldset), which renders the native\n//! `<fieldset>`/`<legend>` pair and is form-specific, `Group` is a lightweight\n//! attribute mapper for any related set of controls (a cluster of buttons\n//! sharing a disabled state, the input + steppers inside a NumberField, etc.).\n//!\n//! See `spec/components/utility/group.md` for the authoritative contract.\n\nuse alloc::string::String;\n\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr};\nuse ars_i18n::Direction;\n\n/// The ARIA role applied to the [`Group`](self) container.\n///\n/// Defaults to [`GroupRole::Group`], a generic grouping role suitable for\n/// related controls. Switch to [`GroupRole::Region`] when the group is a\n/// landmark that should appear in a page summary (an accessible name is then\n/// required by WAI-ARIA), or to [`GroupRole::Presentation`] to render the\n/// container without any grouping semantics while still propagating state via\n/// [`GroupContext`].\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]\npub enum GroupRole {\n    /// `role=\"group\"` — a set of related UI elements not important enough to\n    /// be included in a page summary or table of contents.\n    #[default]\n    Group,\n    /// `role=\"region\"` — a landmark region that is significant enough to be\n    /// listed in a page summary. Requires an accessible name.\n    Region,\n    /// `role=\"presentation\"` — removes the grouping semantics. Children are\n    /// still grouped visually but not semantically.\n    Presentation,\n}\n\n/// Props for the [`Group`](self) component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// Component instance ID. Always emitted on the root element so adapters\n    /// and consumers can reference the container from other attributes\n    /// (`aria-controls`, `aria-describedby`, etc.).\n    pub id: String,\n\n    /// Whether the group and all contained controls are disabled. Propagated\n    /// to descendants via [`GroupContext`] and surfaced on the root as\n    /// `aria-disabled=\"true\"` plus `data-ars-disabled` for styling.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the group is in an invalid state. Propagated to descendants\n    /// via [`GroupContext`] and surfaced on the root as `aria-invalid=\"true\"`\n    /// plus `data-ars-invalid` for styling.\n    pub invalid: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the group is read-only. Propagated to descendants via\n    /// [`GroupContext`] and surfaced on the root as `aria-readonly=\"true\"`\n    /// plus `data-ars-readonly` for styling.\n    pub read_only: bool,\n\n    /// The ARIA role for the group container. Defaults to [`GroupRole::Group`].\n    pub role: GroupRole,\n\n    /// Layout direction for RTL support. When `Some(_)`, the resolved value\n    /// is forwarded to the root element's `dir` attribute. When `None`, the\n    /// element inherits direction from the DOM cascade.\n    pub dir: Option<Direction>,\n}\n\nimpl Props {\n    /// Returns fresh [`Props`] with the documented defaults — equivalent to\n    /// [`Default::default`], offered as the entry point for the builder chain.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Sets the component instance ID.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.id = id.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets whether the group is disabled. Disabled state propagates to\n    /// descendants through [`GroupContext`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn disabled(mut self, value: bool) -> Self {\n        self.disabled = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets whether the group is invalid. Invalid state propagates to\n    /// descendants through [`GroupContext`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn invalid(mut self, value: bool) -> Self {\n        self.invalid = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets whether the group is read-only. Read-only state propagates to\n    /// descendants through [`GroupContext`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn read_only(mut self, value: bool) -> Self {\n        self.read_only = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets the ARIA role of the group container.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn role(mut self, value: GroupRole) -> Self {\n        self.role = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets the layout direction. Pass `Some(direction)` to forward an\n    /// explicit `dir` attribute, or `None` to inherit from the DOM cascade.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn dir(mut self, value: Option<Direction>) -> Self {\n        self.dir = value;\n        self\n    }\n}\n\n/// Context published by [`Group`](self) so descendant components can inherit\n/// `disabled`, `invalid`, and `read_only` state without re-implementing the\n/// propagation themselves.\n///\n/// Adapters MUST provide this value through their framework's context system\n/// (`provide_context` in Leptos, `use_context_provider` in Dioxus). Descendant\n/// components that participate in inheritance should merge each field with\n/// their own props using a logical OR — a component is disabled if either it\n/// or its containing group is disabled.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct GroupContext {\n    /// Whether the containing group is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n    /// Whether the containing group is in an invalid state.\n    pub invalid: bool,\n    /// Whether the containing group is read-only.\n    pub read_only: bool,\n}\n\n/// DOM parts of the [`Group`](self) component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"group\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// The root container element. Renders as a `<div>` carrying the role,\n    /// state attributes, and propagation context.\n    Root,\n}\n\n/// The API for the [`Group`](self) component.\n///\n/// Constructed via [`Api::new`] from [`Props`] and queried via\n/// [`Api::root_attrs`], [`Api::group_context`], or the [`ConnectApi`] dispatch.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\npub struct Api {\n    props: Props,\n}\n\nimpl Api {\n    /// Creates a new `Api` instance from the given props.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn new(props: Props) -> Self {\n        Self { props }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a reference to the underlying [`Props`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn props(&self) -> &Props {\n        &self.props\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the component's instance ID.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(&self) -> &str {\n        &self.props.id\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the [`GroupContext`] that adapters should publish to\n    /// descendants so they can inherit the group's disabled / invalid /\n    /// read-only state.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn group_context(&self) -> GroupContext {\n        GroupContext {\n            disabled: self.props.disabled,\n            invalid: self.props.invalid,\n            read_only: self.props.read_only,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the attributes for the root element.\n    ///\n    /// Always emits the instance `id`, the scope/part data attributes, and a\n    /// `role` derived from [`Props::role`]. State attributes\n    /// (`aria-disabled`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-readonly` plus their\n    /// `data-ars-*` styling counterparts) are emitted whenever the\n    /// corresponding prop is `true`, regardless of the role — WAI-ARIA 1.2\n    /// §5.4 preserves global states on `role=\"presentation\"`, and React Aria\n    /// follows the same convention. When [`Props::dir`] is `Some`, the\n    /// resolved direction is forwarded to the `dir` attribute.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, &self.props.id)\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        let role_str = match self.props.role {\n            GroupRole::Group => \"group\",\n            GroupRole::Region => \"region\",\n            GroupRole::Presentation => \"presentation\",\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, role_str);\n\n        if self.props.disabled {\n            attrs\n                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), \"true\")\n                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-disabled\"), true);\n        }\n        if self.props.invalid {\n            attrs\n                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid), \"true\")\n                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\"), true);\n        }\n        if self.props.read_only {\n            attrs\n                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), \"true\")\n                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-readonly\"), true);\n        }\n        if let Some(dir) = self.props.dir {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Dir, dir.as_html_attr());\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use ars_core::HasId;\n    use insta::assert_snapshot;\n\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n        format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n    }\n\n    fn default_props() -> Props {\n        Props::default()\n    }\n\n    fn region_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().role(GroupRole::Region)\n    }\n\n    fn presentation_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().role(GroupRole::Presentation)\n    }\n\n    fn all_states_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new()\n            .disabled(true)\n            .invalid(true)\n            .read_only(true)\n    }\n\n    fn rtl_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().dir(Some(Direction::Rtl))\n    }\n\n    // ── Props ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_default_is_zero_state() {\n        let p = Props::default();\n\n        assert_eq!(p.id, \"\");\n        assert!(!p.disabled);\n        assert!(!p.invalid);\n        assert!(!p.read_only);\n        assert_eq!(p.role, GroupRole::Group);\n        assert_eq!(p.dir, None);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_builder_round_trips() {\n        let p = Props::new()\n            .id(\"group-build\")\n            .disabled(true)\n            .invalid(true)\n            .read_only(true)\n            .role(GroupRole::Region)\n            .dir(Some(Direction::Rtl));\n\n        assert_eq!(p.id, \"group-build\");\n        assert!(p.disabled);\n        assert!(p.invalid);\n        assert!(p.read_only);\n        assert_eq!(p.role, GroupRole::Region);\n        assert_eq!(p.dir, Some(Direction::Rtl));\n\n        assert_eq!(Props::new(), Props::default());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_builder_setters_are_idempotent_per_field() {\n        // Each setter only writes its own field, and later calls overwrite\n        // earlier ones for the same field.\n        let p = Props::new()\n            .disabled(true)\n            .disabled(false)\n            .invalid(true)\n            .role(GroupRole::Presentation)\n            .role(GroupRole::Region);\n\n        assert!(!p.disabled);\n        assert!(p.invalid);\n        assert_eq!(p.role, GroupRole::Region);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_has_id_derive_round_trips() {\n        let mut p = Props::default().with_id(String::from(\"group-1\"));\n        assert_eq!(HasId::id(&p), \"group-1\");\n\n        p.set_id(String::from(\"group-2\"));\n        assert_eq!(HasId::id(&p), \"group-2\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_clone_and_partial_eq_round_trip() {\n        let original = Props {\n            id: String::from(\"group-clone\"),\n            disabled: true,\n            invalid: false,\n            read_only: true,\n            role: GroupRole::Region,\n            dir: Some(Direction::Rtl),\n        };\n\n        let cloned = original.clone();\n        assert_eq!(cloned, original);\n\n        let mutated = Props {\n            disabled: false,\n            ..original.clone()\n        };\n        assert_ne!(mutated, original);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_role_default_is_group() {\n        assert_eq!(GroupRole::default(), GroupRole::Group);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_and_api_debug_impl_is_non_empty() {\n        let api = Api::new(all_states_props());\n\n        let props_dbg = format!(\"{:?}\", api.props());\n        let api_dbg = format!(\"{api:?}\");\n\n        assert!(props_dbg.contains(\"Props\"), \"Props Debug = {props_dbg}\");\n        assert!(api_dbg.contains(\"Api\"), \"Api Debug = {api_dbg}\");\n    }\n\n    // ── Api accessors ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn api_id_returns_empty_str_for_default_props() {\n        assert_eq!(Api::new(Props::default()).id(), \"\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn api_exposes_props_fields() {\n        let original = Props {\n            id: String::from(\"group-7\"),\n            disabled: true,\n            invalid: false,\n            read_only: true,\n            role: GroupRole::Region,\n            dir: Some(Direction::Ltr),\n        };\n\n        let api = Api::new(original.clone());\n\n        assert_eq!(api.id(), \"group-7\");\n        assert_eq!(api.props(), &original);\n    }\n\n    // ── Role mapping ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_default_role_is_group() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(default_props()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"group\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_role_region_overrides() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(region_props()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"region\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_role_presentation_overrides() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(presentation_props()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"presentation\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn role_branches_produce_different_role_attrs() {\n        // Defensive cross-branch inequality: catches a regression that\n        // accidentally collapses the role mapping (e.g. all variants emitting\n        // the same string).\n        let group_attrs = Api::new(default_props()).root_attrs();\n        let region_attrs = Api::new(region_props()).root_attrs();\n        let presentation_attrs = Api::new(presentation_props()).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_ne!(group_attrs, region_attrs);\n        assert_ne!(group_attrs, presentation_attrs);\n        assert_ne!(region_attrs, presentation_attrs);\n    }\n\n    // ── GroupContext propagation ──────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_default_is_all_false() {\n        let ctx = Api::new(Props::default()).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            ctx,\n            GroupContext {\n                disabled: false,\n                invalid: false,\n                read_only: false,\n            }\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_propagates_disabled() {\n        let ctx = Api::new(Props::new().disabled(true)).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            ctx,\n            GroupContext {\n                disabled: true,\n                invalid: false,\n                read_only: false,\n            }\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_propagates_invalid() {\n        let ctx = Api::new(Props::new().invalid(true)).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            ctx,\n            GroupContext {\n                disabled: false,\n                invalid: true,\n                read_only: false,\n            }\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_propagates_read_only() {\n        let ctx = Api::new(Props::new().read_only(true)).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            ctx,\n            GroupContext {\n                disabled: false,\n                invalid: false,\n                read_only: true,\n            }\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_propagates_all_flags_together() {\n        let ctx = Api::new(all_states_props()).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            ctx,\n            GroupContext {\n                disabled: true,\n                invalid: true,\n                read_only: true,\n            }\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_context_is_independent_of_role() {\n        // Role does not gate context propagation — descendants of a\n        // `role=\"presentation\"` group still see the disabled state.\n        let ctx_group = Api::new(Props::new().disabled(true)).group_context();\n        let ctx_region = Api::new(Props::new().disabled(true).role(GroupRole::Region)).group_context();\n        let ctx_presentation =\n            Api::new(Props::new().disabled(true).role(GroupRole::Presentation)).group_context();\n\n        assert_eq!(ctx_group, ctx_region);\n        assert_eq!(ctx_group, ctx_presentation);\n        assert!(ctx_presentation.disabled);\n    }\n\n    // ── Root state attributes ─────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_disabled_emits_aria_and_data_attrs() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(Props::new().disabled(true)).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-disabled\")), Some(\"true\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_invalid_emits_aria_and_data_attrs() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(Props::new().invalid(true)).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\")), Some(\"true\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_read_only_emits_aria_and_data_attrs() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(Props::new().read_only(true)).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-readonly\")), Some(\"true\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_state_attrs_omitted_when_false() {\n        let attrs = Api::new(Props::default()).root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled)), None);\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), None);\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly)), None);\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-disabled\")), None);\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\")), None);\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-readonly\")), None);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_state_attrs_emit_on_presentation_role() {\n        // WAI-ARIA 1.2 §5.4 preserves global states on `role=\"presentation\"`;\n        // ars-ui follows React Aria parity by emitting them unconditionally.\n        let attrs = Api::new(\n            Props::new()\n                .disabled(true)\n                .invalid(true)\n                .read_only(true)\n                .role(GroupRole::Presentation),\n        )\n        .root_attrs();\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"presentation\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly)), Some(\"true\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_dir_emits_dir_attr() {\n        let rtl_attrs = Api::new(rtl_props()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(rtl_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Dir), Some(\"rtl\"));\n\n        let ltr_attrs = Api::new(Props::new().dir(Some(Direction::Ltr))).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(ltr_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Dir), Some(\"ltr\"));\n\n        let auto_attrs = Api::new(Props::new().dir(Some(Direction::Auto))).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(auto_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Dir), Some(\"auto\"));\n\n        let none_attrs = Api::new(Props::default()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(none_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Dir), None);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn root_id_is_set_to_props_id() {\n        // The spec's §1.2 code example sets `HtmlAttr::Id` unconditionally,\n        // so adapters that consume `root_attrs()` always see a deterministic\n        // attribute set; an empty id maps to an empty value.\n        let empty = Api::new(Props::default()).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(empty.get(&HtmlAttr::Id), Some(\"\"));\n\n        let named = Api::new(Props::new().id(\"my-group\")).root_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(named.get(&HtmlAttr::Id), Some(\"my-group\"));\n    }\n\n    // ── ConnectApi dispatch ───────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn part_attrs_dispatches_root() {\n        let api = Api::new(default_props());\n\n        let attrs = api.part_attrs(Part::Root);\n\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-scope\")), Some(\"group\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-part\")), Some(\"root\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn part_attrs_root_equals_root_attrs() {\n        // Defensive: `ConnectApi` dispatch MUST produce exactly what the\n        // inherent `root_attrs` produces across every output-affecting\n        // combination of props.\n        for role in [GroupRole::Group, GroupRole::Region, GroupRole::Presentation] {\n            for disabled in [false, true] {\n                for invalid in [false, true] {\n                    for read_only in [false, true] {\n                        for dir in [None, Some(Direction::Ltr), Some(Direction::Rtl)] {\n                            let api = Api::new(Props {\n                                id: String::from(\"dispatch\"),\n                                disabled,\n                                invalid,\n                                read_only,\n                                role,\n                                dir,\n                            });\n\n                            assert_eq!(api.part_attrs(Part::Root), api.root_attrs());\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ── Snapshots ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_root_default_snapshot() {\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"group_root_default\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(default_props()).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_root_region_snapshot() {\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"group_root_region\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(region_props()).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_root_presentation_snapshot() {\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"group_root_presentation\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(presentation_props()).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_root_all_states_snapshot() {\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"group_root_all_states\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(all_states_props()).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn group_root_dir_rtl_snapshot() {\n        assert_snapshot!(\n            \"group_root_dir_rtl\",\n            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(rtl_props()).root_attrs())\n        );\n    }\n}\n"
}
